# India Government Employment Source Universe

## Purpose

This is the seed/discovery universe for the Job Operating System's Government Job Discovery layer. It is intentionally designed as a **living source universe**, not a fixed Top-N list. The agent must search these named organisations and then recursively discover their subordinate offices, institutes, laboratories, centres, missions, projects, recruitment pages, PDFs, state bodies, districts and municipal bodies.

## Non-negotiable rule

**The numbers in this document are not a crawl limit.** 250, 1,000, 5,000 or 10,000 are coverage milestones, not maximums. Continue discovering legitimate public employment sources until search dimensions produce diminishing returns. Never fabricate names to reach a numeric threshold.

## Research basis

The named institutional phases were assembled from official government directories and institutional lists, including the National Portal of India directory, CSIR laboratories, ICAR institutions, ICMR institutes, DST autonomous S&T institutions, DBT autonomous institutes, Ministry of Earth Sciences organisations, IIT/NIT recruitment directories and UGC/Ministry of Education university lists. The National Portal explicitly covers ministries, departments, boards/undertakings, district offices, directorates, PSUs/JVs/companies/societies, commissions, missions, statutory/autonomous bodies and institutions. [National Portal](https://www.india.gov.in/directory/web-directory)

## Phase 17 — District / municipal exhaustive expansion

Do not stop at state portals. Enumerate every district and major local-government body from the National Portal directory. For each district search: recruitment, vacancy, notice, engagement, consultant, project, contractual, NHM, District Health Society, DRDA, Collectorate, municipal corporation/council, development authority and district mission pages.

- Every district administration / Collectorate in India

- Every District Health Society

- Every District Rural Development Agency

- Every District Mineral Foundation

- Every municipal corporation

- Every municipal council / municipality

- Every development authority

- Every district-level mission / society / programme


## Phase 18 — Recursive organisation expansion

For every discovered organisation, recursively inspect: attached offices, subordinate offices, regional offices, centres, laboratories, institutes, project offices, missions, societies, boards, authorities, hospitals, colleges, field stations and recruitment cells. A parent organisation being present does NOT mean its child organisations are covered.


## Phase 19 — Recruitment endpoint expansion

For every organisation/domain search all relevant endpoint families: /career, /careers, /jobs, /recruitment, /recruitments, /vacancy, /vacancies, /employment, /engagement, /consultant, /project, /notifications, /notices, /advertisement, /opportunities, /walk-in, /contract, /young-professional, /fellowship, /internship and PDF/document repositories. Also search the site's own search facility and linked notice boards.

## Coverage milestones

- [ ] Phase 1–15 named seed universe loaded into GovernmentSource discovery.

- [ ] Phase 16 state/UT source families expanded into concrete official organisations.

- [ ] Phase 17 every district/local-government source enumerated.

- [ ] Phase 18 recursive child-organisation discovery completed.

- [ ] Phase 19 recruitment endpoint/PDF discovery completed.

- [ ] ≥1,000 unique legitimate employment sources discovered.

- [ ] ≥5,000 if additional legitimate sources continue to be discovered.

- [ ] ≥10,000 only if the public government ecosystem actually yields that many distinct useful sources.

- [ ] No fabricated or duplicate sources used to satisfy a numeric threshold.

- [ ] All discovered sources enter the continuous monitoring queue.

- [ ] All sources have recurring crawl schedules and deadline-aware rechecks.


## Agent instruction

Treat this document as a **seed universe and expansion contract**. Do not interpret it as a fixed list. Search every named organisation, deduplicate against PostgreSQL, discover official domains, recursively discover children, and add legitimate new sources back into the monitoring registry. Continue until all available discovery dimensions have been exhausted. The final database count should reflect real discovered sources, not this file's line count.


# Expansion Phase — 10,000+ Concrete Discovery Targets
This section is deliberately different from the named-organisation list. These are **concrete search/discovery targets**, not claims that every target is a separately incorporated organisation. The agent must resolve each target to an actual official page/domain/office before registering it as a GovernmentSource. This prevents padding the universe with invented organisations.
The target matrix contains 36 State/UT scopes × 280 organisation/hiring combinations = **10,080 additional discovery targets**. Combined with the earlier 2,690 source-universe entries, the document contains more than **12,000 distinct discovery targets**. The official Government of India Integrated Government Online Directory provides the state/UT district universe; the Local Government Directory separately exposes districts, sub-districts, blocks, PRIs and urban bodies. These are the authoritative expansion layers the agent should resolve rather than treating this matrix as a final database.
## Resolution rule
For every target: search the official state/UT portal, IGOD, NIC-hosted domain, department directory, district portal, organisation website, recruitment page and linked PDF repository. If a real endpoint is found, register it. If not found, mark the target unresolved and retry later. Never create a fake GovernmentSource merely because the target exists in this document.

## 10,080 target matrix
1. Andhra Pradesh — State Government Secretariat — recruitment
2. Andhra Pradesh — State Government Secretariat — vacancy
3. Andhra Pradesh — State Government Secretariat — career
4. Andhra Pradesh — State Government Secretariat — careers
5. Andhra Pradesh — State Government Secretariat — jobs
6. Andhra Pradesh — State Government Secretariat — employment
7. Andhra Pradesh — State Government Secretariat — engagement

## Evidence / provenance
- IGOD State/UT District directory: official Government of India directory, hosted by NIC/MeitY; it lists state/UT district entry points and links to district websites. citeturn7view0
- IGOD Uttar Pradesh example: the official directory reports 75 district results and exposes each district's official website plus Sub Districts and Blocks links. citeturn8view0
- Local Government Directory: Government of India Ministry of Panchayati Raj directory exposes downloads for districts, sub-districts, villages, PRIs, urban local bodies, wards and development blocks. citeturn1search3
- ICAR official institution directories demonstrate the parent→institute→NRC→bureau→directorate expansion pattern that should be applied across other government ecosystems. citeturn0search0turn0search1
